**Yes, we can use those credits for Claude’s next LSAT U coding tasks.** Your screenshot shows **$94 remaining**, applied automatically to **cloud sessions**, with an expiry of November 5 at 2:59 AM EST. It excludes Projects and Routines.

You can handle the coding through [Claude Code on the web](https://claude.ai/code), without running Claude locally. Cloud sessions can edit the GitHub repository, run tests, and produce a pull request. [Official documentation](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/claude-code-on-the-web).

The setup is:

1. Click **Start a cloud session**.
2. Select **`vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`**. GitHub already appears connected.
3. Start from the latest **`main`**.
4. Give Claude a clearly bounded task and require tests plus a pull request.
5. I review its changes, verify them, and handle publication.

**Those credits fund Claude’s cloud work.** Lovable credits and the platform’s AI usage are billed separately. Claude’s cloud session also won’t automatically have your local files, browser login, or deployment credentials, so we need to include the relevant plan and instructions in its handoff.

We can use this approach for the next chunk. The navigation release we just finished is already live.

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

Do not hand-edit generated types.\\r\\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\\r\\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Stack And Commands\\r\\n\\r\\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\\r\\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\\r\\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\\r\\n- Build: `npm run build`\\r\\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\\r\\n- Test: `npm run test`\\r\\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\\r\\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\\r\\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Working Rules\\r\\n\\r\\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\\r\\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\\r\\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\\r\\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\\r\\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\\r\\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\\r\\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\\r\\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product Constraints\\r\\n\\r\\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\\r\\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\\r\\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\\r\\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\\r\\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Smart Drill Note\\r\\n\\r\\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification (must not skip)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not \\"done\\" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed \\"fix\\" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow Expectations\\r\\n\\r\\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\\r\\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\\r\\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\\r\\n\\r\\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\\r\\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong \\"Done\\" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Codex Adapter Layout\\r\\n\\r\\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\\r\\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\\r\\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known Gaps\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\\r\\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\\r\\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Agent skills\\r\\n\\r\\n### Issue tracker\\r\\n\\r\\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Triage labels\\r\\n\\r\\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Domain docs\\r\\n\\r\\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 14.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"e89703","wall_time_seconds":8.9108681,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":245,"output":"docs/implementation-plans/2026-10-02-schedule-176-verification.md\\ndocs/implementation-plans/artifacts/2026-10-02-schedule-176/student-1280.png\\ndocs/implementation-plans/artifacts/2026-10-02-schedule-176/student-390.png\\ndocs/implementation-plans/artifacts/2026-10-02-schedule-176/teacher-1280.png\\ndocs/implementation-plans/artifacts/2026-10-02-schedule-176/teacher-390.png\\ndocs/implementation-plans/artifacts/2026-10-02-schedule-176/teacher-dialog-390.png\\nsrc/components/schedule/SessionChainPanel.test.tsx\\nsrc/components/schedule/SessionChainPanel.tsx\\nsrc/components/schedule/TodayAgenda.tsx\\nsrc/components/teacher/schedule/StudentSchedulePanel.tsx\\nsrc/hooks/useSessionChain.test.tsx\\nsrc/hooks/useSessionChain.ts\\nsrc/lib/classroomItems.ts\\nsrc/lib/sessionChain.test.ts\\nsrc/lib/sessionChain.ts\\nsrc/pages/MyWorkNotesPage.test.tsx\\nsrc/pages/MyWorkNotesPage.tsx\\nsrc/pages/Schedule.sessionChain.test.tsx\\nsrc/pages/Schedule.tsx\\nsrc/pages/myWorkNotes.css\\nsrc/test/sessionChainFixtures.ts\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"bf82d7","wall_time_seconds":5.2597113,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":6172,"output":"# LSAT U audit implementation: connect sessions to preparation, recap, and follow-up\\r\\n\\r\\nDate: 2026-10-02\\r\\nParent audit: #165. Decision source: #155. Implementation ticket: #176.\\r\\nRepository: `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`.\\r\\nDelivery: one isolated implementation branch and reviewable pull request. Publication follows independent host verification.\\r\\n\\r\\n## The result students and Joshua need\\r\\n\\r\\nA one-to-one appointment becomes a stable context for the work around that appointment. A student opens Schedule, selects an appointment, and sees three groups: Preparation, Recap, and Follow-up. Each group opens the exact existing saved record. Joshua sees the same appointment and links in the selected-student dossier and can deliberately connect existing owned records to it.\\r\\n\\r\\nStudents must never see another student\'s records or teacher-only, draft, archived, or locked material through this feature. An appointment whose end time has passed does not establish that any linked work was completed. Missing links and failed reads must look different.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis is a substantial, bounded slice of the existing audit. It does not authorize rebuilding the entire platform or closing all remaining tickets. Finish the model, student interface, teacher association controls, Notes integration, regression coverage, and verification documentation together.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Existing work to preserve\\r\\n\\r\\nThe seven-destination navigation, assignment-aware exam access, signed-in Attempts recovery, source diagnostics, protected timed runners, Review adapters, Progress/History, study tools, and weekly settings already have implementations. Do not replace them while building this slice.\\r\\n\\r\\nGitHub main was observed at `124fee3a4f14952c5b05abcf3bedd18dd22d1f18` on 2026-10-02. Start from the latest main, record the actual baseline, and preserve later changes. This historical SHA is orientation, not a requirement to reset the repository.\\r\\n\\r\\nFourteen functional audit tickets and deferred design ticket #178 remain open. A shipped feature is not proof that its full acceptance list passed. This slice contributes to #176 and supports #169/#177; it does not certify those tickets.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Locked decisions\\r\\n\\r\\n1. Reuse the current booking/session source and timezone formatting. Preserve booking, management, cancellation, and rescheduling behavior.\\r\\n2. Identify a booking by the pair `source` and `id`. Known booking sources are `redemption` and `single_session`. The display adapter uses `booking-{source}-{id}`; never treat an arbitrary display string as authoritative ownership.\\r\\n3. Associations are explicit. Never match records by title, nearby dates, student name, or email similarity.\\r\\n4. Existing `classroom_items.metadata_json` is the intended additive association home, subject to verification of the existing authenticated update contract. Preserve `source_table` and `source_id`, which identify assignment/content provenance.\\r\\n5. Link existing records. This slice does not generate preparation, author recaps, assign new exercises, send notifications, or rewrite old work.\\r\\n6. One classroom item has at most one session association in this version. Moving it requires an explicit teacher action showing the old and new appointment. Do not overwrite silently.\\r\\n7. Removing an association removes only this feature\'s metadata. It never deletes the item, assignment, answer, recap, or booking.\\r\\n8. Student and teacher views use one shared parser/projection and exact source-link resolution. Preserve the existing source-specific destination builder.\\r\\n9. Authorization remains server enforced. UI filtering adds correct presentation; it cannot substitute for row permissions.\\r\\n10. No schema, RLS, Edge Function, model, production database, entitlement, or canonical question-content changes in this deliverable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## User stories\\r\\n\\r\\n1. As a student, I can select my appointment and see its assigned preparation before attending.\\r\\n2. As a student, I can open the exact saved preparation record and return to the selected appointment.\\r\\n3. As a student, I can find the published recap from that same appointment after the session.\\r\\n4. As a student, I can open follow-up work without confusing it with preparation.\\r\\n5. As a student, I can distinguish no linked recap from a recap source that failed to load.\\r\\n6. As a student, I can see the appointment in the existing displayed timezone.\\r\\n7. As a student, I retain visible verified links during a failed background refresh, with a retry notice.\\r\\n8. As a student switching accounts, I never see the prior account\'s appointment or content while the next account loads.\\r\\n9. As Joshua, I can select a verified student-owned appointment and associate an existing eligible record with a phase.\\r\\n10. As Joshua, I can see the record\'s title, type, visibility, source, and existing association before saving.\\r\\n11. As Joshua, I receive acknowledgment of a successful save before the UI claims it was linked.\\r\\n12. As Joshua, a failed or uncertain save retains my selection and offers safe recovery.\\r\\n13. As Joshua, I can remove a link without removing the underlying work.\\r\\n14. As Joshua, I can view explicitly labeled teacher-only links that are omitted from the student view.\\r\\n15. As Joshua, I see the same booking identity and student-visible links as the student\'s Schedule and My Work Notes.\\r\\n16. As either user, an elapsed or canceled appointment never creates a false completion claim about work.\\r\\n17. As either user, duplicate titles and appointments on the same day do not merge unrelated records.\\r\\n18. As a keyboard or phone user, I can select appointments, open links, and use teacher controls without horizontal overflow or inaccessible dialogs.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Source facts and hazards already found\\r\\n\\r\\n- `useClassroomItems` scopes its query by student ID, but its comment about unlocked content does not mean the query enforces visibility/status/unlock. Explicitly enforce student presentation eligibility using the existing authoritative item contract.\\r\\n- `StudentSchedulePanel` quick-add calls `classroomNotify`. Do not reuse that notification path to associate an existing record.\\r\\n- `StudentBookingPanel` can return possible email matches separately. Those are not verified student-owned bookings and cannot be association targets.\\r\\n- The calendar adapter can mark an elapsed booked appointment `completed`. This is calendar state, not evidence of work completion.\\r\\n- Existing recaps and calendars are not automatically associated. Legacy items without explicit metadata must remain unlinked.\\r\\n- Do not manufacture a relationship merely to make a demonstration appear complete.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Data contract\\r\\n\\r\\nUse a single versioned metadata member named `session_chain`, unless repository inspection identifies an already established equivalent. If one exists, adopt it and document the exact mapping; do not create a second competing contract.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor a new contract, use these fields:\\r\\n\\r\\n| Field | Meaning | Validation |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| version | Contract version | Exactly 1 |\\r\\n| booking_source | Booking family | redemption or single_session |\\r\\n| booking_id | Original source-row ID | Nonempty valid ID according to that source\'s contract |\\r\\n| phase | Group containing the link | preparation, recap, or follow_up |\\r\\n\\r\\nOwnership is established by the current authenticated actor, selected subject, source-row ownership, and classroom-item ownership. A metadata field containing a student ID is not an authorization grant. Reuse existing `created_by` and item attribution for authorship; do not invent historical author claims. A recap phase must reference a recap-compatible existing item, not relabel an arbitrary drill as a recap.\\r\\n\\r\\nMalformed, unknown-version, foreign, or unresolved associations must not create actionable student links. Teacher view should disclose an invalid or unavailable association without revealing foreign record details. Preserve unrelated metadata keys and nested values on every mutation. Never send a stale full metadata object that can overwrite concurrent changes.\\r\\n\\r\\nBefore implementing the writer, inspect the existing update API for authorization, atomic merge or optimistic concurrency, and acknowledgment. Use an existing supported atomic update/CAS contract. If the current writer cannot preserve concurrent metadata edits safely, do not add an unsafe read-then-write workaround. Complete the read path and interfaces, keep association mutation unavailable with a truthful explanation, and document the exact backend prerequisite for the host. Do not deploy a backend change as a workaround.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Exact interface behavior\\r\\n\\r\\n### Student Schedule\\r\\n\\r\\nKeep the current calendar/agenda and booking controls. Selecting an owned appointment opens or expands a Session work panel within the existing Schedule surface. Its heading displays the existing appointment title, date/time, timezone, and current booking state.\\r\\n\\r\\nBelow the heading, display Preparation, Recap, and Follow-up in that order. Each record displays its title, existing type, author attribution where genuinely available, and existing verified work status where supported. Use Open preparation, Open recap, or Open follow-up as accessible action labels. Include enough title context to distinguish multiple links.\\r\\n\\r\\nEmpty group copy: No preparation linked, No recap linked, or No follow-up linked. Never say completed because a group is empty. Failed source copy identifies what could not load and includes Retry. Initial loading is distinct from empty.\\r\\n\\r\\nKeep verified cached rows visible during background refetch failure for the same actor/subject. Mark their freshness honestly. Disable mutation or actions whose authorization cannot be confirmed by the existing contract. On actor/subject change, immediately clear prior projected data; caching must not display the previous subject.\\r\\n\\r\\nPersist selected appointment with a versioned, validated URL query representation of source and ID. Preserve unrelated query parameters. Browser back and reload restore the same owned appointment. An invalid, unavailable, or foreign selection shows a neutral unavailable state and a way back to the user\'s schedule, without choosing a nearby appointment automatically.\\r\\n\\r\\nCanceled appointments retain their legitimate historical links with a canceled label. Do not treat cancellation as completed work. Disable new linking to canceled appointments. Rescheduled appointments retain links only when the underlying source identity remains the same. If a provider creates a replacement ID, do not infer a transfer.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Teacher dossier\\r\\n\\r\\nPlace the same Session work panel in the existing selected-student Schedule surface. Clearly label the selected student. The teacher sees student-visible links plus explicitly labeled teacher-only items permitted by existing access controls.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor a verified owned, noncanceled appointment, provide Link existing work. The dialog contains phase selection and an eligible item picker scoped to the selected student. Show item type, visibility, and any current appointment association. Require a deliberate selection and save; do not auto-select the first record.\\r\\n\\r\\nIf the item is already linked to another appointment, show both appointment identities and require explicit Move link confirmation. Saving updates only the association and waits for acknowledgment. During save, prevent duplicate submission. On failure retain the selection; distinguish unacknowledged save from acknowledged save followed by refresh failure.\\r\\n\\r\\nProvide Remove link with confirmation explaining that the record remains available in My Work. Acknowledgment precedes removal from the panel. If the server may have committed but the response was lost, re-read authoritative association state before repeating an unsafe write. Reuse idempotent or CAS semantics where available.\\r\\n\\r\\nChanging the selected student closes the dialog, clears draft selection, invalidates the mutation epoch, and prevents late results/toasts from affecting the next student. A late callback may invalidate the original subject\'s cache, but cannot alter the current subject UI.\\r\\n\\r\\n### My Work Notes and recaps\\r\\n\\r\\nFor eligible explicitly linked records, display a session context link containing the appointment\'s date/time and source-backed identity. Open Schedule with that exact selection. Preserve existing record/source links, author/type labels, filters, and return behavior.\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not move unlinked notes into a guessed session. Do not surface hidden records to students through this adapter. Empty for students. */\\r\\n  items: ClassroomItem[];\\r\\n  bookingsLoading: boolean;\\r\\n  itemsLoading: boolean;\\r\\n  /** No booking data could be read for this subject. */\\r\\n  bookingsFailed: boolean;\\r\\n  /** No item data could be read; links are unknown, not empty. */\\r\\n  itemsFailed: boolean;\\r\\n  /** A background refresh failed; the rows shown are from an earlier read. */\\r\\n  stale: boolean;\\r\\n  retry: () => void;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface QueryLike<T> {\\r\\n  data: T | undefined;\\r\\n  isLoading: boolean;\\r\\n  isError: boolean;\\r\\n  refetch: () => unknown;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function deriveSessionChainState(input: {\\r\\n  audience: Audience;\\r\\n  subjectId: string | null;\\r\\n  bookingQuery: QueryLike<SessionBooking[]>;\\r\\n  itemQuery: QueryLike<ClassroomItem[]>;\\r\\n  now?: Date;\\r\\n}): SessionChainState {\\r\\n  const { audience, subjectId, bookingQuery, itemQuery } = input;\\r\\n  // Data is trusted only for the subject it was read for. Queries are keyed by\\r\\n  // subject, so a new subject starts with no data rather than the last one\'s.\\r\\n  const bookings = subjectId ? bookingQuery.data ?? [] : [];\\r\\n  const items = subjectId ? (itemQuery.data ?? []).filter((item) => item.student_id === subjectId) : [];\\r\\n  const projection = subjectId\\r\\n    ? projectSessionChain({ items, bookings, subjectId, audience, now: input.now })\\r\\n    : { byBooking: new Map(), issues: [] };\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    audience,\\r\\n    subjectId,\\r\\n    bookings,\\r\\n    projection,\\r\\n    items: audience === \'teacher\' ? items : [],\\r\\n    bookingsLoading: !!subjectId && bookingQuery.isLoading && bookingQuery.data === undefined,\\r\\n    itemsLoading: !!subjectId && itemQuery.isLoading && itemQuery.data === undefined,\\r\\n    bookingsFailed: bookingQuery.isError && bookingQuery.data === undefined,\\r\\n    itemsFailed: itemQuery.isError && itemQuery.data === undefined,\\r\\n    stale: (bookingQuery.isError && bookingQuery.data !== undefined) || (itemQuery.isError && itemQuery.data !== undefined),\\r\\n    retry: () => {\\r\\n      void bookingQuery.refetch();\\r\\n      void itemQuery.refetch();\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Student Schedule and Notes: the signed-in student\'s own appointments and items. */\\r\\nexport function useStudentSessionChain(): SessionChainState {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const subjectId = user?.id ?? null;\\r\\n  const booking = useStudentBooking();\\r\\n  const items = useClassroomItems();\\r\\n  const bookingData = useMemo(\\r\\n    () => (booking.data ? (booking.data.bookings ?? []).map(toSessionBooking).filter((b): b is SessionBooking => !!b) : undefined),\\r\\n    [booking.data],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  return deriveSessionChainState({\\r\\n    audience: \'student\',\\r\\n    subjectId,\\r\\n    bookingQuery: { data: bookingData, isLoading: booking.isLoading, isError: booking.isError, refetch: booking.refetch },\\r\\n    itemQuery: { data: items.data, isLoading: items.isLoading, isError: items.isError, refetch: items.refetch },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ntype AdminSingleSession = {\\r\\n  source?: string;\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  start_at: string;\\r\\n  end_at: string;\\r\\n  status: string;\\r\\n  meet_url?: string | null;\\r\\n  user_id?: string | null;\\r\\n  association?: string;\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Bookings Joshua can verify as this student\'s. Possible email matches are never read into this list.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function fetchTeacherBookings(studentId: string): Promise<SessionBooking[]> {\\r\\n  const [redemptions, admin] = await Promise.all([\\r\\n    supabase\\r\\n      .from(\'booking_pass_redemptions\')\\r\\n      .select(\'id,start_at,end_at,status,meet_url,booking_passes!inner(student_id)\')\\r\\n      .eq(\'booking_passes.student_id\', studentId)\\r\\n      .order(\'start_at\', { ascending: false }),\\r\\n    supabase.functions.invoke(\'booking-pass-admin\', { body: { action: \'get_for_student\', student_id: studentId } }),\\r\\n  ]);\\r\\n  if (redemptions.error) throw redemptions.error;\\r\\n  if (admin.error) throw new Error(admin.error.message);\\r\\n  const adminData = admin.data as { error?: string; single_session_bookings?: AdminSingleSession[] } | null;\\r\\n  if (adminData?.error) throw new Error(adminData.error);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const rows: SessionBooking[] = [];\\r\\n  for (const row of (redemptions.data ?? Retrying must re-read first. */\\r\\n  | { kind: \'unknown\'; message: string }\\r\\n  | { kind: \'unavailable\' };\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction currentLinkState(item: ClassroomItem): ExpectedLink {\\r\\n  const parsed = parseSessionChain(item.metadata_json);\\r\\n  if (parsed.kind === \'invalid\') return \'invalid\';\\r\\n  return parsed.kind === \'valid\' ? parsed.link : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction matches(actual: ExpectedLink, expected: ExpectedLink): boolean {\\r\\n  if (actual === \'invalid\' || expected === \'invalid\') return actual === expected;\\r\\n  return sameLink(actual, expected);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction message(error: unknown): string {\\r\\n  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : \'The change could not be saved.\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Change one item\'s association and nothing else.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Reads the authoritative row, refuses if its association is not what the\\r\\n * teacher was shown, then writes `metadata_json` with only `session_chain`\\r\\n * changed, conditioned on the row\'s `updated_at`. A lost reply is settled by\\r\\n * re-reading the row, never by repeating the write blind.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function commitSessionLink(input: {\\r\\n  itemId: string;\\r\\n  studentId: string;\\r\\n  expected: ExpectedLink;\\r\\n  next: SessionChainLink | null;\\r\\n}): Promise<CommitOutcome> {\\r\\n  const fresh = await getClassroomItem(input.itemId);\\r\\n  if (!fresh || fresh.student_id !== input.studentId) return { kind: \'unavailable\' };\\r\\n  const current = currentLinkState(fresh);\\r\\n  if (current !== \'invalid\' && sameLink(current, input.next)) return { kind: \'saved\', item: fresh };\\r\\n  if (!matches(current, input.expected)) return { kind: \'conflict\', current };\\r\\n\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const written = await updateClassroomItemMetadataIfUnchanged({\\r\\n      id: fresh.id,\\r\\n      studentId: input.studentId,\\r\\n      expectedUpdatedAt: fresh.updated_at,\\r\\n      metadata: withSessionChain(fresh.metadata_json, input.next),\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    if (written) return { kind: \'saved\', item: written };\\r\\n    const after = await getClassroomItem(input.itemId);\\r\\n    if (!after || after.student_id !== input.studentId) return { kind: \'unavailable\' };\\r\\n    return { kind: \'conflict\', current: currentLinkState(after) };\\r\\n  } catch (error) {\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const after = await getClassroomItem(input.itemId);\\r\\n      if (!after || after.student_id !== input.studentId) return { kind: \'unavailable\' };\\r\\n      const state = currentLinkState(after);\\r\\n      if (state !== \'invalid\' && sameLink(state, input.next)) return { kind: \'saved\', item: after };\\r\\n      if (after.updated_at === fresh.updated_at) return { kind: \'not_saved\', message: message(error) };\\r\\n      return { kind: \'conflict\', current: state };\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      return { kind: \'unknown\', message: message(error) };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type WriterResult =\\r\\n  | (CommitOutcome & { refreshFailed?: boolean })\\r\\n  /** The selected student changed while the write was in flight. */\\r\\n  | { kind: \'superseded\' };\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Context-bound writer for one selected student. A result that arrives after\\r\\n * the student changed is reported as superseded, so the caller cannot apply\\r\\n * it to the next student\'s panel; the original student\'s cache is still\\r\\n * refreshed.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function useSessionLinkWriter(studentId: string | null) {\\r\\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\\r\\n  const epoch = useRef(0);\\r\\n  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const inFlight = useRef(false);\\r\\n\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    epoch.current += 1;\\r\\n    inFlight.current = false;\\r\\n    setPending(false);\\r\\n  }, [studentId]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const save = useCallback(\\r\\n    async (itemId: string, expected: ExpectedLink, next: SessionChainLink | null): Promise<WriterResult | null> => {\\r\\n      if (!studentId || inFlight.current) return null;\\r\\n      const mine = epoch.current;\\r\\n      const subject = studentId;\\r\\n      inFlight.current = true;\\r\\n      setPending(true);\\r\\n      let outcome: nothing was written.\\r\\n        outcome = { kind: \'not_saved\', message: message(error) };\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      let refreshFailed = false;\\r\\n      if (outcome.kind !== \'not_saved\') {\\r\\n true;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (mine !== epoch.current) return { kind: \'superseded\' };\\r\\n      inFlight.current = false;\\r\\n      setPending(false);\\r\\n      return { (\\r\\n        <>\\r\\n          <LinkDialog\\r\\n            open={linkOpen}\\r\\n            onOpenChange={setLinkOpen}\\r\\n            booking={booking}\\r\\n            state={state}\\r\\n            writer={writer}\\r\\n            studentName={studentName}\\r\\n            onResult={(result) => setFeedback(feedbackFor(result, \'linked\', state.bookings))}\\r\\n          />\\r\\n          <AlertDialog open={!!removing} onOpenChange={(next) => { if (!next && !writer.pending) setRemoving(null); }}>\\r\\n            <AlertDialogContent>\\r\\n              <AlertDialogHeader>\\r\\n                <AlertDialogTitle>Remove link?</AlertDialogTitle>\\r\\n                <AlertDialogDescription>\\r\\n                  {removing ? `“${removing.item.title}” will no longer be listed under ${PHASE_LABEL[removing.phase]} for this session.` : \'\'} The record itself stays available in My Work. Nothing is deleted.\\r\\n                </AlertDialogDescription>\\r\\n              </AlertDialogHeader>\\r\\n              <AlertDialogFooter>\\r\\n                <AlertDialogCancel disabled={writer.pending}>Keep link</AlertDialogCancel>\\r\\n                <AlertDialogAction onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); void confirmRemove(); }} disabled={writer.pending}>\\r\\n                  {writer.pending ? <Loader2 className=\\"mr-1.5 h-4 w-4 animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none\\" /> : null}Remove link\\r\\n                </AlertDialogAction>\\r\\n              </AlertDialogFooter>\\r\\n            </AlertDialogContent>\\r\\n          </AlertDialog>\\r\\n        </>\\r\\n      ) : null}\\r\\n    </>,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Teacher dossier: the selected student\'s verified appointments and the\\r\\n * Session work panel for the one Joshua picks. Selection, dialogs and any\\r\\n * in-flight save are bound to `studentId`; switching students resets them.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function TeacherSessionWork({ studentId, studentName }: { studentId: string; studentName: string | null }) {\\r\\n  const state = useTeacherSessionChain(studentId);\\r\\n  const writer = useSessionLinkWriter(studentId);\\r\\n  const [selected, setSelected] = React.useState<BookingIdentity | null>(null);\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => { setSelected(null); }, [studentId]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"@container/schedule space-y-3\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4\\">\\r\\n        <h2 className=\\"text-sm font-semibold\\">Session work · {studentName || \'Selected student\'}</h2>\\r\\n        <p className=\\"mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">Verified appointments for this student only. Possible email matches are not listed.</p>\\r\\n        {state.bookingsLoading ? (\\r\\n          <p role=\\"status\\" className=\\"mt-3 flex items-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\\"><Loader2 className=\\"mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none\\" />Loading appointments…</p>\\r\\n        ) : state.bookingsFailed ? (\\r\\n          <div role=\\"alert\\" className=\\"mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm text-destructive\\"><CircleAlert className=\\"h-4 w-4\\" />Appointments could not load.<Button size=\\"sm\\" variant=\\"outline\\" onClick={state.retry}>Retry</Button></div>\\r\\n        ) : state.bookings.length === 0 ? (\\r\\n          <p className=\\"mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">No verified appointments for this student.</p>\\r\\n        ) : (\\r\\n          <ul className=\\"mt-3 max-h-72 space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto pr-1\\" aria-label=\\"Verified appointments\\">\\r\\n            {state.bookings.map((booking) => {\\r\\n              const key = bookingKey(booking);\\r\\n              const active = !!selected && bookingKey(selected) === key;\\r\\n              const linked = groupsFor(state.projection, booking);\\r\\n              const count = SESSION_PHASES.reduce((n, p) => n + linked[p].length, 0);\\r\\n              return (\\r\\n                <li key={key}>\\r\\n                  <button\\r\\n                    type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                    aria-pressed={active}\\r\\n                    onClick={() => setSelected(active ? null : { source: booking.source, id: booking.id })}\\r\\n                    className={cn(\'flex w-full flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring\', active ? \'border-primary bg-primary/5\' : \'border-border hover:bg-accent/40\')}\\r\\n                  >\\r\\n                    <span className=\\"min-w-0\\">{formatAppointment(booking)}</span>\\r\\n                    <span className=\\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">{bookingStateLabel(booking)}{count ? ` · ${count} linked` : \'\'}</span>\\r\\n                  </button>\\r\\n                </li>\\r\\n              );\\r\\n            })}\\r\\n          </ul>\\r\\n        )}\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n      {selected ? (\\r\\n        <SessionChainPanel\\r\\n          state={state}\\r\\n          selected={selected}\\r\\n          onBack={() => setSelected(null)}\\r\\n          backLabel=\\"Close\\"\\r\\n          studentName={studentName}\\r\\n          writer={writer}\\r\\n        />\\r\\n      ) : null}\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

The server already enforces the first three for a\\r\\n * student; this keeps an admin previewing a student route, or a cache that\\r\\n * outlived a visibility change, from presenting more than the student sees.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function isStudentEligible(item: ClassroomItem, studentId: string, now = new Date()): boolean {\\r\\n  if (item.student_id !== studentId) return false;\\r\\n  if (!item.visible_to_student) return false;\\r\\n  if (item.status === \'draft\' || item.status === \'archived\') return false;\\r\\n  if (item.type === \'bootcamp_module\') return true;\\r\\n  if (!item.unlock_at) return true;\\r\\n  const unlock = new Date(item.unlock_at).getTime();\\r\\n  return !Number.isNaN(unlock) && unlock <= now.getTime();\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Why a record is withheld from the student, for the teacher\'s label. */\\r\\nexport function teacherOnlyReason(item: ClassroomItem, now = new Date()): string | null {\\r\\n  if (item.status === \'draft\') return \'Draft\';\\r\\n  if (item.status === \'archived\') return \'Archived\';\\r\\n  if (!item.visible_to_student) return \'Hidden from student\';\\r\\n  if (item.type !== \'bootcamp_module\' && item.unlock_at && new Date(item.unlock_at).getTime() > now.getTime()) {\\r\\n    return \'Locked until unlock date\';\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Items Joshua may pick in Link existing work for this student. */\\r\\nexport function isLinkCandidate(item: ClassroomItem, studentId: string): boolean {\\r\\n  return item.student_id === studentId && phasesFor(item.type).length > 0 && item.status !== \'archived\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ── Projection ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type Audience = \'student\' | \'teacher\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface LinkedRecord {\\r\\n  item: ClassroomItem;\\r\\n  phase: SessionPhase;\\r\\n  route: string | null;\\r\\n  /** Set in the teacher view when the student cannot see this record. */\\r\\n  teacherOnly: string | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type PhaseGroups = Record<SessionPhase, LinkedRecord[]>;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface AssociationIssue {\\r\\n  itemId: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  problem: \'invalid_metadata\' | \'appointment_unavailable\' | \'phase_mismatch\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface SessionChainProjection {\\r\\n  byBooking: Map<string, PhaseGroups>;\\r\\n  /** Teacher only. Never populated for a student. */\\r\\n  issues: AssociationIssue[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function emptyGroups(): PhaseGroups {\\r\\n  return { preparation: [], recap: [], follow_up: [] };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction byCreatedThenId(a: LinkedRecord, b: LinkedRecord) {\\r\\n  const t = Date.parse(a.item.created_at) - Date.parse(b.item.created_at);\\r\\n  return t !== 0 && !Number.isNaN(t) ? t : a.item.id.localeCompare(b.item.id);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function projectSessionChain(input: {\\r\\n  items: ClassroomItem[];\\r\\n  bookings: SessionBooking[];\\r\\n  subjectId: string;\\r\\n  audience: Audience;\\r\\n  now?: Date;\\r\\n}): SessionChainProjection {\\r\\n  const { items, bookings, subjectId, audience } = input;\\r\\n  const now = input.now ?? new Date();\\r\\n  const owned = new Map(bookings.map((booking) => [bookingKey(booking), booking]));\\r\\n  const byBooking = new Map<string, PhaseGroups>();\\r\\n  const issues: AssociationIssue[] = [];\\r\\n  const isTeacher = audience === \'teacher\';\\r\\n\\r\\n  for (const item of items) {\\r\\n    // Foreign rows are dropped silently in both views: no title, no id.\\r\\n    if (item.student_id !== subjectId) continue;\\r\\n    const studentEligible = isStudentEligible(item, subjectId, now);\\r\\n    if (!isTeacher && !studentEligible) continue;\\r\\n\\r\\n    const parsed = parseSessionChain(item.metadata_json);\\r\\n    if (parsed.kind === \'none\') continue;\\r\\n    if (parsed.kind === \'invalid\') {\\r\\n      if (isTeacher) issues.push({ itemId: item.id, title: item.title, problem: \'invalid_metadata\' });\\r\\n      continue;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const { link } = parsed;\\r\\n    const key = bookingKey({ source: link.booking_source, id: link.booking_id });\\r\\n    if (!owned.has(key)) {\\r\\n      if (isTeacher) issues.push({ itemId: item.id, title: item.title, problem: \'appointment_unavailable\' });\\r\\n      continue;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (!phaseAccepts(link.phase, item.type)) {\\r\\n      if (isTeacher) issues.push({ itemId: item.id, title: item.title, problem: \'phase_mismatch\' });\\r\\n      continue;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const groups = byBooking.get(key) ?? emptyGroups();\\r\\n    groups[link.phase].push({\\r\\n      item,\\r\\n      phase: link.phase,\\r\\n      route: resolveItemRoute(item),\\r\\n      teacherOnly: isTeacher && !studentEligible ? teacherOnlyReason(item, now) ?? \'Not visible to student\' : null,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    byBooking.set(key, groups);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  for (const groups of byBooking.values()) {\\r\\n    for (const phase of SESSION_PHASES) groups[phase].sort(byCreatedThenId);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return { byBooking, issues };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function groupsFor(projection: SessionChainProjection, identity: BookingIdentity | null): PhaseGroups {\\r\\n  if (!identity) return emptyGroups();\\r\\n  return projection.byBooking.get(bookingKey(identity)) ?? emptyGroups();\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ── URL selection ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const SESSION_PARAM = \'session\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** `v1.<source>.<id>` — versioned so a future format can be told apart. */\\r\\nexport function encodeSessionParam(identity: BookingIdentity): string {\\r\\n  return `v1.${identity.source}.${identity.id}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type DecodedSessionParam =\\r\\n  | { kind: \'absent\' }\\r\\n  | { kind: \'invalid\' }\\r\\n  | { kind: \'valid\'; identity: BookingIdentity };\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function decodeSessionParam(raw: string | null | undefined): DecodedSessionParam {\\r\\n  if (raw === null || raw === undefined || raw === \'\') return { kind: \'absent\' };\\r\\n  const match = /^v1\\\\.([a-z_]+)\\\\.([^.]+)$/.exec(raw);\\r\\n  if (!match) return { kind: \'invalid\' };\\r\\n  const [, source, id] = match;\\r\\n  if (!isBookingSource(source) || !isValidBookingId(source, id)) return { kind: \'invalid\' };\\r\\n  return { kind: \'valid\', identity: { source, id } };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Next query string with only the session selection changed. */\\r\\nexport function withSessionParam(params: URLSearchParams, identity: BookingIdentity | null): URLSearchParams {\\r\\n  const next = new URLSearchParams(params);\\r\\n  if (identity) next.set(SESSION_PARAM, encodeSessionParam(identity));\\r\\n  else next.delete(SESSION_PARAM);\\r\\n  return next;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The typed booking pair behind a calendar booking entry, read from the\\r\\n * adapter\'s `booking_source` and `source_id`, never from its display id.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function identityFromBookingItem(item: Pick<ClassroomItem, \'source_id\' | \'metadata_json\'>): BookingIdentity | null {\\r\\n  const source = item.metadata_json?.booking_source;\\r\\n  if (!isBookingSource(source) || !isValidBookingId(source, item.source_id)) return null;\\r\\n  return { source, id: item.source_id };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function scheduleHrefFor(identity: BookingIdentity): string {\\r\\n  return `/schedule?${SESSION_PARAM}=${encodeURIComponent(encodeSessionParam(identity))}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Appointment date/time in the runtime timezone, the Schedule\'s existing contract. */\\r\\nexport function formatAppointment(booking: Pick<SessionBooking, \'startAt\' | \'endAt\'>): string {\\r\\n  const start = new Date(booking.startAt);\\r\\n  const date = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { weekday: \'short\', month: \'short\', day: \'numeric\', year: \'numeric\' }).format(start);\\r\\n  const time = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { hour: \'numeric\', minute: \'2-digit\' });\\r\\n  const end = booking.endAt ? new Date(booking.endAt) : null;\\r\\n  const span = end && !Number.isNaN(end.getTime()) ? `${time.format(start)} – ${time.format(end)}` : time.format(start);\\r\\n  return `${date}, ${span}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function displayTimeZone(): string {\\r\\n  return Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst TYPE_LABEL: Partial<Record<ClassroomItemType, string>> = {\\r\\n  drill: \'Drill\',\\r\\n  homework: \'Homework\',\\r\\n  activity: \'Activity\',\\r\\n  bootcamp_module: \'Bootcamp module\',\\r\\n  practice_test: \'PrepTest\',\\r\\n  note: \'Note\',\\r\\n  session_recap: \'Session recap\',\\r\\n  smartboard_recap: \'Board recap\',\\r\\n  announcement: \'Announcement\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function itemTypeLabel(type: ClassroomItemType): string {\\r\\n  return TYPE_LABEL[type] ?? type.replace(/_/g, \' \');\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The item\'s own stored work status. Never derived from the calendar. */\\r\\nexport function itemStatusLabel(item: ClassroomItem): string | null {\\r\\n  switch (item.status) {\\r\\n    case \'assigned\': return \'Assigned\';\\r\\n    case \'in_progress\': return \'In progress\';\\r\\n    case \'completed\': return \'Completed\';\\r\\n    case \'draft\': return \'Draft\';\\r\\n    case \'archived\': return \'Archived\';\\r\\n    default: return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Read-only review complete. Two material UI/spec findings:

1. **P2: Notes hides item-source failures silently.** [MyWorkNotesPage.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-191/src/pages/MyWorkNotesPage.tsx:125) returns `null` when `chain.itemsFailed` is true. A failed classroom-item read makes session links disappear without an error or retry, although booking failure displays an alert. This violates the spec’s requirement to distinguish missing links from failed reads. The new failure test covers only `bookingsFailed`. Handle both failure flags explicitly and test item failure separately.

2. **P2: Canceled historical sessions cannot be selected normally from student Schedule.** [Schedule.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-191/src/pages/Schedule.tsx:223) uses `useBookingScheduleItems` for its calendar/agenda selector; that adapter filters to `status === 'booked'`. The new panel understands canceled bookings, but students cannot discover/select them through Schedule. Notes supplies another path only if a linked record is a note/recap, so canceled sessions containing preparation/follow-up drills have no discoverable history path. Add a canceled/history appointment selector based on the verified chain bookings without changing cancellation/booking controls.

Covered every changed UI, CSS, fixture, and UI-test file: SessionChainPanel and tests, Schedule and route tests, TodayAgenda, StudentSchedulePanel, MyWorkNotesPage and tests, myWorkNotes.css, sessionChainFixtures. Also traced shared read/writer state, projection, booking adapter and existing ownership source to assess integration.

Other observations:
- Exact phase/source links, manual item/phase selection, explicit move/remove confirmation, teacher-only labels, canceled panel state, and normal empty/loading/error states are implemented clearly.
- Teacher subject switches key-remount the component, so no confirmed cross-subject UI leak found in this integration.
- Responsive evidence is an isolated fixture harness, not actual full Schedule/dossier/Notes-shell browser evidence; the verification document discloses that limitation correctly.
- I ran no edits, mutations, commits, browser actions, or credit/reset controls.
